## What UK customers say

### Complaints

| Theme | Share | Reviews | Quote | Fix |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Fabric basket hard to reshape after flat packing | 6% | 4 | “very hard to get the fabric to stay straight without wrinkles” | Ship fabric/woven planters pre-formed or rolled with a stiffener insert, not folded flat; add reshaping instructions. |
| No drainage holes | 6% | 4 | “my only gripe is that it doesn't have any drainage” | Clearly label as a cachepot (no drainage) in the listing, or offer a variant with drainage holes and plug. |
| Thin / cheaply made wooden planters | 5% | 3 | “Looks as though they will rot quicker than more sturdy planters” | Specify thicker timber and pre-line with a waterproof liner; state board thickness on the spec sheet. |
| Attached drainage saucer hard to detach | 3% | 2 | “this is fiddly (hence 4*)to detach for watering” | Redesign saucer with an easy twist-off or loose-fit tray, and test detachment before shipping. |
| Finish worn or imperfect | 3% | 2 | “lots of places on drainage saucer where the gold part is worn away” | Add finish QC and a sealed lacquer/colour-fast coating so gold and stain don't wear or leach. |

## Spec improvements

1. Offer a version with drainage holes and a rubber plug, and clearly label the no-hole version as a cachepot (decorative outer pot) on the spec sheet.
2. Replace any fixed saucer with a loose-fit or easy twist-off tray, and test that it comes off easily before shipping.
3. Seal the hammered gold finish with a clear lacquer coat and add a finish check at final QC, so the gold does not wear away.
4. Wrap each planter and saucer separately in soft, non-scratch packaging so the gold finish arrives without rub marks.

## Compliance notes

- UK: products must carry a UK-responsible person/importer address on packaging (GPSR equivalent under UK product safety rules).
- Candle holders and lanterns: include fire-safety warnings for use with candles.
- Food-contact items (bowls, trays, barware): brass must be lined/lacquered and meet UK food-contact rules.
- CETA zero duty needs a valid proof of origin (statement on origin / certificate) — verify before quoting.

## Assumptions

| Assumption | Value |
|---|---|
| UK VAT | 20% |
| Retailer markup | 2.2× |
| Importer / wholesaler markup | 1.8× |
| Freight, insurance and clearance (share of FOB) | 15% |
| Import duty from India | 0% |
| Exchange rate (GBP→INR, live) | 127.44 |
